high score 8.4

apk-debuggable

static

Application is debuggable; increases reverse-engineering risk.

<sandbox>\AndroGoat.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 8.4 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 7.5 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-489 (Active debug code)
  • NIST: CM-7 (Least functionality); RA-5 (Vulnerability scanning)
  • OWASP: MASVS-RESILIENCE-1 (App not debuggable in release); M9 (Reverse engineering (Mobile Top 10))
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-169 (Footprinting)
  • CISA: SecureBuild (Disable debug in production (CISA))
  • NSA: MobileApp (Release builds: no debug (NSA))
How to fix
Set android:debuggable=false for release builds (default when using a release build type). Do not ship debug APKs to production.
How to revalidate
Build a release APK and re-run --sast; apk-debuggable should be absent when the manifest flag is false/unset for release.

apk-allow-backup

static

allowBackup is true; app data may be backed up unencrypted.

<sandbox>\AndroGoat.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 5.5 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 5.3 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:N/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-200 (Information exposure)
  • NIST: SC-28 (Protection of information at rest)
  • OWASP: MASVS-STORAGE-2 (Sensitive data not in backups); M2 (Insecure data storage)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-37 (Retrieve embedded sensitive data)
  • CISA: Backup (Restrict backup of sensitive app data)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Control backup exposure (NSA))
How to fix
Set android:allowBackup=false, or use a backup rules / data extraction rules file that excludes sensitive data.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast; apk-allow-backup should clear when allowBackup is false or appropriately restricted.

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 5.8 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 6.5 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:L/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-926 (Improper export of Android component)
  • NIST: AC-4 (Information flow enforcement); AC-17 (Remote access)
  • OWASP: MASVS-PLATFORM-1 (Platform interaction / exported components); M8 (Security misconfiguration / IPC)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-180 (Exploiting software via component vulnerability)
  • CISA: LeastPriv (Least privilege; protect exported components)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Secure IPC and exports (NSA))

How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast; apk-exported-unprotected should clear for components that are no longer exported without permission.

dex-ssl-trust

static

Trust-all / disabled certificate validation heuristic. (seen: "()Ljavax/net/ssl/X509TrustManager;)

<sandbox>\AndroGoat.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 8.8 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 8.1 AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-295 (Improper certificate validation)
  • NIST: SC-8 (Transmission confidentiality); SC-23 (Session authenticity)
  • OWASP: MASVS-NETWORK-2 (Certificate pinning / secure channel); M3 (Insecure communication)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-94 (Man in the middle)
  • CISA: ValidateCerts (Validate TLS certificates; no trust-all)
  • NSA: TLS (Proper certificate validation (NSA))
How to fix
Remove custom TrustManager / HostnameVerifier implementations that accept all certificates. Use the system trust store; add certificate pinning only with a maintained pin set.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast (and --dynamic if you use the Frida SSL check). Expect ssl-trust / trust-all rule_ids to clear.

dex-storage-sharedprefs

static

Uses SharedPreferences; ensure sensitive data is encrypted. (seen: *Landroid/content/SharedPreferences$Editor;)

<sandbox>\AndroGoat.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 0.8 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 4.0 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:N/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-312 (Cleartext storage of sensitive information)
  • NIST: SC-28 (Protection of information at rest)
  • OWASP: MASVS-STORAGE-2 (Sensitive data encrypted at rest); M2 (Insecure data storage)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-37 (Retrieve embedded sensitive data)
  • CISA: EncryptAtRest (Encrypt sensitive data at rest)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Encrypt app storage (NSA mobile))
How to fix
Do not store secrets in plaintext SharedPreferences. Use EncryptedSharedPreferences or Keystore-backed storage.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast. Expect dex-storage-sharedprefs hits related to the sensitive keys to clear.

dex-hardcoded-password

static

Possible password/credential reference (low confidence; high FP). (seen: password)

<sandbox>\AndroGoat.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 2.5 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 5.3 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:L/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-798 (Hard-coded credentials)
  • NIST: IA-5 (Authenticator management)
  • OWASP: MASVS-AUTH-4 (No sensitive data in credentials); M1 (Improper credential usage (Mobile Top 10))
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-114 (Credential stuffing)
  • CISA: StrongAuth (Use strong authentication; no hardcoded secrets)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Mobile app: avoid hardcoded credentials (NSA mobile guidance))
How to fix
Remove hardcoded passwords from app binaries and resources. Use platform secret stores (Android Keystore / iOS Keychain) or authenticate via a backend; never ship production credentials in the APK/IPA/JAR.
How to revalidate
Rebuild the artifact, then re-run: python cli.py <path> --sast -o json. Expect this rule_id to be absent (or only leftover false-positive token hits at low/info). Compare finding counts for the same path.

dex-hardcoded-password

static

Possible password/credential reference (low confidence; high FP). (seen: Change Password)

<sandbox>\DVACv1.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 2.5 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 5.3 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:L/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-798 (Hard-coded credentials)
  • NIST: IA-5 (Authenticator management)
  • OWASP: MASVS-AUTH-4 (No sensitive data in credentials); M1 (Improper credential usage (Mobile Top 10))
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-114 (Credential stuffing)
  • CISA: StrongAuth (Use strong authentication; no hardcoded secrets)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Mobile app: avoid hardcoded credentials (NSA mobile guidance))
How to fix
Remove hardcoded passwords from app binaries and resources. Use platform secret stores (Android Keystore / iOS Keychain) or authenticate via a backend; never ship production credentials in the APK/IPA/JAR.
How to revalidate
Rebuild the artifact, then re-run: python cli.py <path> --sast -o json. Expect this rule_id to be absent (or only leftover false-positive token hits at low/info). Compare finding counts for the same path.
low score 2.5

dex-hardcoded-secret

static

Possible secret reference (low confidence; high FP). (seen: Failed to copy secret file)

<sandbox>\DVACv1.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 0.8 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 5.5 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:L/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-798 (Hard-coded credentials); CWE-321 (Hard-coded cryptographic key)
  • NIST: SC-28 (Protection of information at rest); IA-5 (Authenticator management)
  • OWASP: MASVS-CRYPTO-1 (Standard crypto; no hardcoded keys); M2 (Insecure data storage (Mobile Top 10))
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-116 (Excavation)
  • CISA: NoSecrets (Secrets in secure vault; not in code)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Mobile: protect cryptographic keys)
How to fix
Remove embedded secrets/crypto keys from the binary. Load keys from a secure backend or hardware-backed store at runtime.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-run --sast; this rule_id should clear when the secret patterns are gone.

dex-storage-sharedprefs

static

Uses SharedPreferences; ensure sensitive data is encrypted. (seen: *Landroid/content/SharedPreferences$Editor;)

<sandbox>\DVACv1.apk

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 0.8 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 4.0 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:N/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-312 (Cleartext storage of sensitive information)
  • NIST: SC-28 (Protection of information at rest)
  • OWASP: MASVS-STORAGE-2 (Sensitive data encrypted at rest); M2 (Insecure data storage)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-37 (Retrieve embedded sensitive data)
  • CISA: EncryptAtRest (Encrypt sensitive data at rest)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Encrypt app storage (NSA mobile))
How to fix
Do not store secrets in plaintext SharedPreferences. Use EncryptedSharedPreferences or Keystore-backed storage.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast. Expect dex-storage-sharedprefs hits related to the sensitive keys to clear.
info score 0.8

Application is debuggable; increases reverse-engineering risk.

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 8.4 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 7.5 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:N

  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-489 (Active debug code)
  • NIST: CM-7 (Least functionality); RA-5 (Vulnerability scanning)
  • OWASP: MASVS-RESILIENCE-1 (App not debuggable in release); M9 (Reverse engineering (Mobile Top 10))
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-169 (Footprinting)
  • CISA: SecureBuild (Disable debug in production (CISA))
  • NSA: MobileApp (Release builds: no debug (NSA))

  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-798 (Hard-coded credentials)
  • NIST: IA-5 (Authenticator management)
  • OWASP: MASVS-AUTH-4 (No sensitive data in credentials); M1 (Improper credential usage (Mobile Top 10))
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-114 (Credential stuffing)
  • CISA: StrongAuth (Use strong authentication; no hardcoded secrets)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Mobile app: avoid hardcoded credentials (NSA mobile guidance))

<sandbox>\ovaa-debug.apk

  • Severity score: 8.8 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 8.1 AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-295 (Improper certificate validation)
  • NIST: SC-8 (Transmission confidentiality); SC-23 (Session authenticity)
  • OWASP: MASVS-NETWORK-2 (Certificate pinning / secure channel); M3 (Insecure communication)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-94 (Man in the middle)
  • CISA: ValidateCerts (Validate TLS certificates; no trust-all)

  • OWASP: MASVS-NETWORK-2 (Certificate pinning / secure channel); M3 (Insecure communication)

Taxonomy & scoring

  • Severity score: 5.5 / 10
  • CVSS-estimate: 5.3 AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:N/A:N
    rule-based estimate, not NVD CVSS for a CVE
  • CVE: null (CVE IDs require component/version SCA; not invented here)
  • CWE: CWE-200 (Information exposure)
  • NIST: SC-28 (Protection of information at rest)
  • OWASP: MASVS-STORAGE-2 (Sensitive data not in backups); M2 (Insecure data storage)
  • CAPEC: CAPEC-37 (Retrieve embedded sensitive data)
  • CISA: Backup (Restrict backup of sensitive app data)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Control backup exposure (NSA))
How to fix
Set android:allowBackup=false, or use a backup rules / data extraction rules file that excludes sensitive data.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast; apk-allow-backup should clear when allowBackup is false or appropriately restricted.

  • CAPEC: CAPEC-180 (Exploiting software via component vulnerability)
  • CISA: LeastPriv (Least privilege; protect exported components)
  • NSA: MobileApp (Secure IPC and exports (NSA))
How to fix
Add a permission (signature/custom) on exported components, or set android:exported=false if not needed by other apps.
How to revalidate
Rebuild and re-scan with --sast; apk-exported-unprotected should clear for components that are no longer exported without permission.
